DataTables 捕獲階段穿透技術 (Capture-Phase Penetration)： * 使用原生 addEventListener('click', handler, true)，在 DataTables 執行 stopPropagation 吞掉點擊事件前 0.001 毫秒穿透捕獲 <tr> 代號。 4. 同源原則 (Same-Origin)：所有看板統一於相同連接埠 (如 Port 8888 或 44311) 運行以保障通訊暢通。 📌 模組三：全知大寬表與量化特徵工程 (View_AI_Omni_Context) SQL Server 於 0.1 秒內預先完成時間維度與微結構「特徵工程」，徹底消滅 AI 數學幻覺： 1. 時空座標：a.日期 AS 資料日期 (時序精準對齊)。 2. 股本基因標籤：>500億 [巨型權值股] ｜ >50億 [中型主力股] ｜ <=50億 [小型輕盈飆股]。 3. 動態乖離戰術判定 (解決賣飛飆股痛點)：小型飆股乖離率在 30% 內判定為 [小型飆股軋空主升段 (容忍高乖離)]，大型股 >15% 則給予過熱警告。 4. 一年期 3D AMT 價值區微結構 (VWAP 模擬)： * 短線 20 日主力成本 (POC_20) ｜ 波段 60 日主力成本 (POC_60) ｜ 長線 240 日年線主力成本 (POC_240)。 * 空間判定：[突破一年與一季均價，萬里無雲主升段] / [價值區間箱型震盪] / [長線空頭深水區]。 5. 籌碼共振熱度與四維分項穿透： * 輸出明確四維明細：價格金叉分 | OBV金叉分 | 外資金叉分 | 投信金叉分 (總分 32)。 * 負數 (如 -14/32) 精準指向空頭死叉與法人連續拋售，AI 不再抱怨缺乏分項數據。 6. 物理去重裝甲：內建 ROW_NUMBER() OVER(PARTITION BY a.代號 ORDER BY a.日期 DESC) as dedup_rn 確保全市場每檔股票輸出絕對唯一。 📌 模組四：純英數符號・零切換 Telegram 旗艦 V4.5 (zen_ai_bot1.py) 徹底終結手機操盤切換中文輸入法之困擾，鍵盤永遠鎖定數字與符號頁面： * 3443? ➔ 👑 旗艦全知戰報 (大寬表 ✕ 雙表格 ✕ 戰令四要素) * 3443?1 ➔ ⚡ 60M 伏兵轉強 (分時動能/KD金叉/當日攻防) * 3443?2 ➔ 🧱 3D AMT 價值區 (20/60/240日主力成本/突圍空間) * 3443?3 ➔ 🛡️ 3-Way 離場勝率 (5/10/20MA勝率PK/凱利下注%) * 3443?4 ➔ 💎 籌碼集中度 (共振熱度/法人集中度) * 3443?? (或 K, TOP) ➔ 🌟 60M 金叉轉強黑馬榜 (連股號都免打) * 內建 extract_pure_code 正規提取，徹底杜絕【2454?】代號誤判 Bug。 📌 模組五：第 100 號部隊 ✕ 雙引擎防截斷調度 (ai_omni_explainer.py) * 三重大腦階梯陣列： - 第一階梯 (Google 原廠直連)：gemini-1.5-flash (定海神針/永不503) ➔ gemini-3.8-flash ➔ gemini-3.5-flash-lite ➔ gemini-3.1-pro-preview (4 組金鑰輪詢抗 429/503)。 - 第二階梯 (OpenRouter 雲端備援)：openrouter/free ➔ llama-3.3-70b ➔ nemotron-3.5-lightning (已剔除 404 下架廢棄模型)。 - 第三階梯 (本地 Ollama 7B 實體保底)：qwen2.5:7b 本地斷網接管，保證 100% 絕不出現「全數繁忙」！ * 篇幅配速令與解鎖 8192 Token： - call_gemini_official 解鎖 maxOutputTokens: 8192。 - Prompt 注入篇幅配速令，確保【實戰戰術指令單】雙表格與【👑 總監一句話戰令】100% 完整產出不被腰斬。 * 總監戰令四要素公式：【核心動作定性】+【具體數字生死價位】+【資金成數上限】+【一句克服人性的紀律】。 * 頁尾時間戳記：解盤時間: YYYY/MM/DD HH:MM (實際產出大腦名稱)。 📌 模組六：四網大一統廣播中樞 (zen_broadcaster.py) * 整合 Apprise (TG + LINE + Discord) ✕ Playwright (Facebook 社團動態發文)。 * 深度自動探針 auto_detect_tokens()：自動穿透掃描現有腳本，無痛繼承 Google 原廠金鑰、OpenRouter 金鑰、Telegram Token、Discord Webhook 與 LINE Token。 * 支援 --code 動態傳參，調用 View_AI_Omni_Context 產出雙表格全知戰報，一鍵四網齊發。 📌 模組七：Facebook 社團自動發文徹底破障 (fb_post_stock.py) * Session 永久固化：經由原生純淨 Chrome 登入 2FA 固化 Session 至 C:\test_c\chrome_profile。 * 徹底移除 --no-sandbox 參數，消滅黃色警告條，防止 Arkose Labs (MatchKey) 安全沙盒崩潰白屏。 * 原生 JS 穿透點擊 (page.evaluate)：精準鎖定「留個言吧……」喚醒彈窗；以 page.keyboard.insert_text 注入戰報；以原生 JS 強制擊發「發佈」按鈕，徹底消滅 React 半透明遮罩引起的 intercepts pointer events 30秒超時報錯！ 📌 模組八：99 部隊母艦 (master_cockpit.py) 與機房級隱形無人值守 * 頂部全域雷達鎖定器 (紫色火控列)：輸入股號一鍵四網齊發，支援自由體驗區多腳本參數動態注入 (STOCK_ARG_ROUTER)。 * 內建 Port 8551 跨域 API 伺服器：支援 TradingView 戰情三室實體按鈕跨域呼叫發射廣播。 * RWD 響應式佈局 (ResponsiveRow)：手機端自動垂直折疊，徹底解決手機載入時因寬度溢出導致的 RenderFlex 黑屏。 * 機房級隱形守護程序： - start_zen_core.bat：自動釋放 8550 埠 (防 Errno 10048)，以 start /B 背景靜默執行母艦與 ngrok。 - silent_run.vbs：以純英文檔名呼叫批次檔 (0 參數徹底隱藏黑視窗)，置入 shell:startup 達成開機全自動常駐。 - 一鍵停止母艦.bat：隨時一鍵強制終止背景母艦與 ngrok。 📌 模組九：每日盤後大一統自動化排程架構 1. 每天 15:30 (Windows 工作排程器)： * 自動執行 auto_aftermarket_derivatives.bat： - python sync_market_derivatives.py (抓取期交所真實個股期貨與可轉債行情入庫)。 - python sync_etf_wantgoodate.py (更新高配息 ETF 歷史行情)。 2. 每天 16:30 (SQL Server Agent 排程 ZenAI_Daily_AfterMarket_Master)： * 自動執行大一統流水線 sp_RunDailyAfterMarket_Master (階段 1~6)： - [9/10] sp_Refresh_ETF_DividendRank：全自動動態轉入 ETF_DailyPriceDate 最新資料 (修復小數點字串轉 BIGINT 之 NULL 陷阱)。 - [10/10] sp_Refresh_CB_SSF_Monitor：連動 amountchange 真實收盤價，區分有期貨標的 (正負價差/真實口數) 與無期貨標的 (NULL/無發行期貨)。 ================================================================================ 🚨 十九項終極除錯防線條約 (V15.0 終極憲法) ================================================================================ 1. 括號防護律：AS 欄位別名凡含 (%), (天), 中文, 符號或空格，100% 強制加上中括號 [別名]。 2. 無 CTE WITH 條約：WebMethod 與 ExecuteSQL 不支援 CTE WITH，前端範本全改用衍生子查詢 (SELECT ...) M。 3. 一字漲跌停防衛：(最高 - 最低) = 0 時，加 ISNULL(..., 0) 防 NULL 傳播。 4. Unicode N 前綴：所有中文文字比對 (如 LIKE N'%雙破共振%') 100% 加上 N 前綴。 5. 日期轉型防溢位律：資料庫日期帶有 .000000 浮點數時，必須先 CAST(日期 AS INT)，再進行 VARCHAR(8) 與 TRY_CONVERT。 6. 回測策略隔離律：嚴禁無差別 TRUNCATE！必用 DELETE ... WHERE 回測編號 = X；插入總表必開啟 SET IDENTITY_INSERT ... ON。 7. 視窗函數別名禁止律：嚴禁在同一層 SELECT 中將剛取的欄位別名直接用於後面的 OVER() 視窗函數。 8. Flutter 官方直通車律：Android 打包分身 App 僅改 applicationId，嚴禁修改 namespace；根絕 ClassNotFoundException 閃退。 9. Gemini 多金鑰防限額律：多金鑰輪詢池必須採用「不同 Google 帳號」輪詢；HTTP Header 帶上 "x-goog-api-key"。 10. Playwright 靜默穿透與防卡白屏律：守護腳本必須 headless=True；遇 Arkose Labs 驗證嚴禁自動化初次登入，必用原生 Chrome 登入 2FA 固化 Session；嚴禁攜帶 --no-sandbox 破壞安全沙盒。 11. 雙平台故障隔離：廣播推播時 Telegram 優先發送，Facebook 發文必須包覆獨立 try...except 與 clean_fb_profile_lock() 破鎖機制。 12. 真·回測頸線不破律：幾何 1:1 測幅之回測必須在頸線「上方」0%~3.5% (Close >= Neckline * 0.99)。 13. Flet 1.0 物件大寫與 RWD 防崩潰律：Border, Padding 必須大寫；子進程必須強制帶 -u 與 PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1；佈局必須採用 ResponsiveRow 防手機寬度溢出黑屏。 14. 母艦單一長輪詢與沙盒授權律：同一組 TG Bot Token 嚴禁同時運行多個腳本防 409 Conflict；母艦實施沙盒免密體驗與實戰密碼鎖 (2157) 雙軌制；連接埠衝突時執行 taskkill 瞬態釋放。 15. TradingView 工業版鎖定與繪圖純淨律：前端鎖定 lightweight-charts@4.2.1 穩定版；均線強制 priceLineVisible: false 根絕彩色橫向虛線；標記僅支援 arrowDown/circle。 16. 三屏聯動同源與事件捕獲穿透律：跨看板聯動必須同源 (同 Port 8888 或 44311)；穿透 DataTables 阻擋必用 addEventListener('click', handler, true) 於 Capture 階段攔截 <tr> 代號。 17. Android Intent 穿透與 HTTPS 直連律：手機直通母艦嚴禁使用 intent:// 特殊協議 (防 ERR_UNKNOWN_URL_SCHEME)，必須採用標準 HTTPS 平滑跳轉；VBScript 隱形啟動之檔名與路徑絕對禁止使用中文！ 18. 純英數代號正規清洗律與大寬表物理去重律：TG 機器人提取股號必須嚴格使用 re.search(r'([0-9]{4,6})', text) 防代號帶問號 Bug；大寬表必內建 ROW_NUMBER() ... dedup_rn = 1 物理防胞胎；動態乖離率結合股本規模標籤防賣飛飆股。 19. 大模型長文本防截斷與原廠 8K 解鎖律 (NEW)： * 調用 Google 原廠 API 必須強制宣告 maxOutputTokens: 8192，提示詞必注入「篇幅配速令」，確保雙表格與總監戰令 100% 完整收尾不被腰斬。 * 必須建立「Google原廠 ➔ OpenRouter最新在線 ➔ 本地Ollama 7B」三重防禦裝甲，徹底消滅 404、429、503 與「全數繁忙」！ * 總監一句話戰令必須嚴格落實四要素：【核心動作定性】+【具體數字生死價位】+【資金成數上限】+【一句克服人性的紀律】。 ================================================================================ 🏁 記憶傳承 V15.0 終極大滿貫封存完畢！天下武功，唯快不破，唯穩不敗，無人值守神機大成！ ================================================================================ 記憶傳承+SQL常用欄位+所有的預存程序+python用途+範本內容(excelsql2.html內) https://weizen99.github.io/androidapp/memory/最新記憶傳承.html https://weizen99.github.io/androidapp/memory/常用欄位.html https://weizen99.github.io/androidapp/memory/所有的預存程序.html https://weizen99.github.io/androidapp/memory/test_c下的python用途.html https://weizen99.github.io/androidapp/memory/excelsql2_範本內容.html https://weizen99.github.io/androidapp/memory/主要網頁.html

    <!-- 十九項終極除錯防線條約 -->
    <section class="card">
        <div class="card-header law">🚨 十九項終極除錯防線條約 (V15.0 終極憲法)</div>
        <ol class="laws-list">
            <li><strong>括號防護律：</strong>AS 欄位別名凡含 (%), (天), 中文, 符號或空格，100% 強制加上中括號 <code>[別名]</code>。</li>
            <li><strong>無 CTE WITH 條約：</strong>WebMethod 與 ExecuteSQL 不支援 CTE WITH，前端範本全改用衍生子查詢 <code>(SELECT ...) M</code>。</li>
            <li><strong>一字漲跌停防衛：</strong>(最高 - 最低) = 0 時，加 <code>ISNULL(..., 0)</code> 防 NULL 傳播。</li>
            <li><strong>Unicode N 前綴：</strong>所有中文文字比對 (如 <code>LIKE N'%雙破共振%'</code>) 100% 加上 N 前綴。</li>
            <li><strong>日期轉型防溢位律：</strong>資料庫日期帶有 .000000 浮點數時，必須先 <code>CAST(日期 AS INT)</code>，再進行 VARCHAR(8) 與 TRY_CONVERT。</li>
            <li><strong>回測策略隔離律：</strong>嚴禁無差別 TRUNCATE！必用 <code>DELETE ... WHERE 回測編號 = X</code>；插入總表必開啟 <code>SET IDENTITY_INSERT ... ON</code>。</li>
            <li><strong>視窗函數別名禁止律：</strong>嚴禁在同一層 SELECT 中將剛取的欄位別名直接用於後面的 OVER() 視窗函數。</li>
            <li><strong>Flutter 官方直通車律：</strong>Android 打包分身 App 僅改 applicationId，嚴禁修改 namespace；根絕 ClassNotFoundException 閃退。</li>
            <li><strong>Gemini 多金鑰防限額律：</strong>多金鑰輪詢池必須採用「不同 Google 帳號」輪詢；HTTP Header 帶上 "x-goog-api-key"。</li>
            <li><strong>Playwright 靜默穿透與防卡白屏律：</strong>守護腳本必須 headless=True；遇 Arkose Labs 驗證嚴禁自動化初次登入，必用原生 Chrome 登入 2FA 固化 Session；嚴禁攜帶 --no-sandbox 破壞安全沙盒。</li>
            <li><strong>雙平台故障隔離：</strong>廣播推播時 Telegram 優先發送，Facebook 發文必須包覆獨立 try...except 與 <code>clean_fb_profile_lock()</code> 破鎖機制。</li>
            <li><strong>真·回測頸線不破律：</strong>幾何 1:1 測幅之回測必須在頸線「上方」0%~3.5% (Close &gt;= Neckline * 0.99)。</li>
            <li><strong>Flet 1.0 物件大寫與 RWD 防崩潰律：</strong>Border, Padding 必須大寫；子進程必須強制帶 -u 與 PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1；佈局必須採用 ResponsiveRow 防手機寬度溢出黑屏。</li>
            <li><strong>母艦單一長輪詢與沙盒授權律：</strong>同一組 TG Bot Token 嚴禁同時運行多個腳本防 409 Conflict；母艦實施沙盒免密體驗與實戰密碼鎖 (2157) 雙軌制；連接埠衝突時執行 taskkill 瞬態釋放。</li>
            <li><strong>TradingView 工業版鎖定與繪圖純淨律：</strong>前端鎖定 <code>lightweight-charts@4.2.1</code> 穩定版；均線強制 <code>priceLineVisible: false</code> 根絕彩色橫向虛線；標記僅支援 arrowDown/circle。</li>
            <li><strong>三屏聯動同源與事件捕獲穿透律：</strong>跨看板聯動必須同源 (同 Port 8888 或 44311)；穿透 DataTables 阻擋必用 <code>addEventListener('click', handler, true)</code> 於 Capture 階段攔截 <code>&lt;tr&gt;</code> 代號。</li>
            <li><strong>Android Intent 穿透與 HTTPS 直連律：</strong>手機直通母艦嚴禁使用 intent:// 特殊協議 (防 ERR_UNKNOWN_URL_SCHEME)，必須採用標準 HTTPS 平滑跳轉；VBScript 隱形啟動之檔名與路徑絕對禁止使用中文！</li>

報告長官！一眼看出致命病因，這是一個非常經典的「命令列參數錯位（Argument Misalignment）與旗標吞噬 Bug」！ --- ### 🔍 案情精準診斷（Why & What） 1. 圖一 Telegram 破案點： Telegram 訊息顯示：`股號：--stock`。 2. 病因根源： * 母艦 (`master_cockpit.py`) 在呼叫腳本時，傳遞了帶有旗標的參數：`python chart_screenshot.py --stock 2330`（或者當上方輸入框為空時，只送出了 `--stock`）。 * 但 `chart_screenshot.py` 內部很可能是用簡單的 `sys.argv[1]` 來接收股號，結果 `sys.argv[1]` 剛好就是字串 `"--stock"`，而不是後面的股號 `2330`！ * 甚至直接違反了憲法第 18 條【純英數代號正規清洗律】，沒有透過正則 `\d{4,6}` 驗證是否為合法台股代號，直接把 `"--stock"` 當成股號印出並執行！ --- ### 🛠️ 終極徹底修復方案 我們需要給 `chart_screenshot.py` 裝上「萬能參數兼容解析器」與「預設防呆裝甲」，無論是直接傳 `2330`、傳 `--stock 2330`、傳 `--stock=2330`，甚至是母艦留空傳入時，都能 100% 精準識別！ #### 步驟一：修改 `C:\test_c\chart_screenshot.py` 請打開 `C:\test_c\chart_screenshot.py`，找到最前面的參數讀取段落（通常在 `import` 後面或 `main()` 開頭），替換成以下「無敵防呆參數解析函數」：
python
import sys
import re

def get_target_stock_code():
    """
    萬能股號提取器：
    相容以下所有呼叫格式：
    1. python chart_screenshot.py 2330
    2. python chart_screenshot.py --stock 2330
    3. python chart_screenshot.py --stock=2330
    4. python chart_screenshot.py --code 2330
    5. 母艦留空或只傳入 --stock 時，自動防呆回退為 2330
    """
    raw_args = sys.argv[1:]
    
    # 1. 優先掃描所有參數，尋找是否含有 4~6 碼純數字 (符合台股代號特徵)
    for i, arg in enumerate(raw_args):
        # 排除 --stock, --code 等旗標本身
        if arg.startswith('-'):
            # 如果是 --stock=2330 格式
            if '=' in arg:
                val = arg.split('=', 1)[1]
                match = re.search(r'\b\d{4,6}\b', val)
                if match:
                    return match.group(0)
            # 如果是 --stock 2330 格式 (取下一個參數)
            elif i + 1 < len(raw_args) and not raw_args[i + 1].startswith('-'):
                match = re.search(r'\b\d{4,6}\b', raw_args[i + 1])
                if match:
                    return match.group(0)
        else:
            # 直接傳遞純股號：如 chart_screenshot.py 2330
            match = re.search(r'\b\d{4,6}\b', arg)
            if match:
                return match.group(0)
                
    # 2. 如果全都沒有找到合法 4~6 碼股號，給予預設值 (2330)
    return "2330"

# 🌟 全域獲取純淨股號 (保證 100% 絕對是純數字，絕不會是 '--stock')
STOCK_CODE = get_target_stock_code()
請確保後續所有產生圖表、查詢 SQL、以及發送 Telegram 訊息中，都統一使用此 `STOCK_CODE`。 --- #### 步驟二：檢查母艦 `C:\test_c\master_cockpit.py` 在母艦程式中，檢查傳參路由字典 `STOCK_ARG_ROUTER`（或執行按鈕事件）：

    "chart_screenshot.py": "--stock",  # 或是直接使用純參數
    "ai_omni_explainer.py": "--code",
    # ... 其他腳本
}
同時檢查母艦組裝指令時，如果長官在頂部「輸入股號」輸入框未填寫時，不要只送出 `["--stock"]`，而要補上預設值：

# 組裝指令範例：

    flag = STOCK_ARG_ROUTER[script_name]
    cmd = [sys.executable, script_path, flag, target_stock]
else:
    cmd = [sys.executable, script_path, target_stock]

AS float)` 會炸 8114）。 * 解決方針：在該 SQL 語句中使用 `TRY_CAST(欄位 AS float)` 或 `TRY_CONVERT(float, 欄位)` 取代直接 `CAST`，即可徹底避開 8114 異常！ --- 依照步驟一更新 `chart_screenshot.py` 的參數解析函數後，無論在母艦點擊「立即試玩」，或是在頂部輸入任何股號發射，Telegram 接收到的訊息將立即恢復為標準的：

import re

stock = "2330"  # 預設值

    match = re.search(r'\b\d{4,6}\b', arg)
    if match:
        stock = match.group(0)
        break

報告長官！抓到了！ ### 🔍 異常原因破案： 截圖中的日誌： > `🎯 全域雷達鎖定：已自動為 【chart_screenshot.py】注入目標參數 None 2317` > `❌ 執行異常: expected str, bytes or os.PathLike object, not NoneType` 這是因為母艦中直接把 `None` 塞進了指令列表 `cmd`（例如 `cmd.extend([flag, stock])`），而 Python 的 `subprocess` 要求指令列表內每一個元素都必須是字串 (`str`)，不能有 `None`！ --- ### 💡 最完美解法：把母艦改回 `"--stock"`，升級 `chart_screenshot.py` 這樣最省事、最乾淨！母艦完全符合統一規範，而 `chart_screenshot.py` 也能雙向兼容（手動打數字可以、母艦傳 `--stock` 也可以）！ #### 步驟 1：把母艦 `master_cockpit.py` 改回 `"--stock"` 在 `C:\test_c\master_cockpit.py` 中，將 `STOCK_ARG_ROUTER` 改回原本的設定：

python
import sys
import re

# 🌟 智慧參數解析：自動相容「純數字 2454」與「母艦 --stock 2454」
stock = "2330"  # 預設值
for arg in sys.argv[1:]:
    # 只要符合 4~6 碼純數字 (過濾掉 --stock、--code 等旗標)
    if re.match(r'^\d{4,6}$', arg):
        stock = arg
        break

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
# 🏆 本檔案為【純圖片背景自動截圖工具 V8.60】，支援雙 Y 軸最新圖表強刷新、防快取時間戳記與智慧按鈕點擊！
import os
import sys
import time
import datetime
import asyncio
import requests
import pyodbc
import re

# ==========================================
# 🛠️【設定區】
# ==========================================
LINE_TOKEN = "SJV5ZwRl6qyUwOdM7LZNlYX/qoEnQk5nOzzpIFhwTFMTIH+d10+TtLs5fFhELKxB20BCDAG1O8jiYHqaK45vyYqyqMTVeWv0qDc7IDSRVXQ1Wrw1RCrUzZxLukxD6KSxdvdFGoaFhLZBIswi8GeLCgdB04t89/1O/w1cDnyilFU="
LINE_USER_ID = "Ubeff0e73d9e40c884055eadf8cc43293"
NGROK_DOMAIN = "smokiness-supremacy-agonizing.ngrok-free.dev"

# 資料庫連線字串
SERVER = "pc048000110"
DATABASE = "stockchange"
DRIVER = "{SQL Server}"
CONN_STR = f'DRIVER={DRIVER};SERVER={SERVER};DATABASE={DATABASE};Trusted_Connection=yes'

TG_TOKEN = "8769891212:AAEPDZeQIMQEJYbDbYyIecYOV06szBRcbEw"   
TG_CHAT_ID = "-1003926900701"  # 🟢 對齊「Zen AI 量化戰情室」群組 ID

def send_image_to_line(photo_filename, stock_code, target_line_id):
    image_url = f"https://{NGROK_DOMAIN}/static/{photo_filename}"
    url = "https://api.line.me/v2/bot/message/push"
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {LINE_TOKEN}"
    }
    payload = {
        "to": target_line_id,
        "messages": [{"type": "image", "originalContentUrl": image_url, "previewImageUrl": image_url}]
    }
    try:
        print(f"📲 正在透過 LINE Bot 發送【雙 Y 軸強刷新版】圖表連結: {image_url}")
        requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=15)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ 發送至 LINE 失敗:", e)
    return False

def send_image_to_telegram(photo_path, stock_code, tg_chat_id):
    # 優先推送至「Zen AI 量化戰情室」群組 (-1003926900701)
    active_chat_id = tg_chat_id if (tg_chat_id and str(tg_chat_id).startswith("-")) else TG_CHAT_ID
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TG_TOKEN}/sendPhoto"
    try:
        with open(photo_path, 'rb') as f:
            files = {'photo': f}
            data = {
                'chat_id': active_chat_id, 
                'caption': f"📊 戰情室一鍵聯動圖表 (雙Y軸極致版)\n股號: {stock_code}\n產生時間: {datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y/%m/%d %H:%M')}"
            }
            requests.post(url, files=files, data=data, timeout=15)
            return True
    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ TG 發送圖片失敗:", e)
    return False

def clean_old_screenshots(static_dir, current_filename):
    try:
        for f in os.listdir(static_dir):
            if f.startswith("chart_") and f.endswith(".png"):
                if f != current_filename:
                    os.remove(os.path.join(static_dir, f))
    except: pass

async def capture_and_push(stock_code, start_date, target_line_id):
    try:
        with pyodbc.connect(CONN_STR, timeout=5) as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("EXEC stockchangedate.dbo.sp_CalcIntradayStockLive @StockCode = ?", (stock_code.strip(),))
            conn.commit()
        print(f"⚡ [Python 預先計算] 股號 {stock_code} 盤中隨動計算已在資料庫端完成。")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ Python 預先計算失敗: {e}")

    # 🛠️【100% 快取摧毀】：帶上動態亂數時間戳記
    timestamp_now = int(time.time() * 1000)
    url = f"https://zen2965.duckdns.org:8888/excelimport/excelsql2.html?stock={stock_code.strip()}&start={start_date.strip()}&_v={timestamp_now}"
    
    static_dir = r"C:\test_c\static"
    os.makedirs(static_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
    photo_filename = f"chart_{stock_code}_{timestamp_now}.png"
    output_path = os.path.join(static_dir, photo_filename)
    
    print(f"🚀 Playwright 前往最新網址 (防快取): {url}")
    
    from playwright.async_api import async_playwright
    async with async_playwright() as p:
        browser = await p.chromium.launch(headless=True)
        
        context = await browser.new_context(
            viewport={"width": 1400, "height": 1300}, 
            ignore_https_errors=True,
            bypass_csp=True,
            extra_http_headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate", "Pragma": "no-cache"}
        )
        page = await context.new_page()
        
        try:
            await page.goto(url, wait_until="networkidle", timeout=60000)
            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
            
            # 自動填表
            await page.fill("input[id='keyword']", "")
            await page.fill("input[id='keyword']", stock_code)
            await page.fill("input[id='起2']", "")
            await page.fill("input[id='起2']", start_date)
            
            # 盤中/盤後時段智慧按鈕判定
            print("⚡ 正在進行智慧時段判定並模擬點擊聯動按鈕...")
            btn_clicked = False

            now_time = datetime.datetime.now().time()
            is_trading_hours = datetime.time(8, 30) <= now_time <= datetime.time(14, 0)

            # A. 盤中時段 (08:30 ~ 14:00)
            if is_trading_hours:
                print("☀️ 當前為盤中交易時段，優先點擊 id='btn_query_live' (盤中即時隨動)...")
                btn_live = page.locator("#btn_query_live")
                if await btn_live.count() > 0 and await btn_live.is_visible():
                    print("🎯 🎯 成功定位 id='btn_query_live'，正在點擊...")
                    await btn_live.click()
                    btn_clicked = True
                
                if not btn_clicked:
                    btn_live_text = page.locator("button:has-text('即時')")
                    if await btn_live_text.count() > 0:
                        print("🎯 找到包含 '即時' 文字的按鈕，正在點擊...")
                        await btn_live_text.first.click()
                        btn_clicked = True

            # B. 盤後歷史時段
            if not btn_clicked:
                print("🌙 當前為盤後歷史時段，點擊 id='btn_query_post' (盤後歷史)...")
                btn_post = page.locator("#btn_query_post")
                if await btn_post.count() > 0 and await btn_post.is_visible():
                    print("🎯 🎯 成功定位 id='btn_query_post'，正在點擊...")
                    await btn_post.click()
                    btn_clicked = True

            if not btn_clicked:
                btn_history = page.locator("button:has-text('歷史')")
                if await btn_history.count() > 0:
                    print("🎯 找到包含 '歷史' 的按鈕，正在點擊...")
                    await btn_history.first.click()
                    btn_clicked = True

            if not btn_clicked:
                btn_old = page.locator("button:has-text('聯動')")
                if await btn_old.count() > 0:
                    print("🎯 找到包含 '聯動' 的舊版按鈕，正在點擊...")
                    await btn_old.first.click()
                    btn_clicked = True

            # C. 終極防線：JS 強制點擊
            if not btn_clicked:
                print("⚠️ 未找到任何常規按鈕，啟動瀏覽器 JS 強制尋找並點擊按鈕...")
                target_keyword = '即時' if is_trading_hours else '歷史'
                await page.evaluate("""
                    (keyword) => {
                        const btn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button, input[type="button"], input[type="submit"], a'))
                            .find(el => el.textContent.includes(keyword) || el.textContent.includes('聯動'));
                        if (btn) btn.click();
                    }
                """, target_keyword)

            await asyncio.sleep(4.5)

            # 淨化畫面
            print("🧹 正在淨化畫面，去除上方 SQL 面板與資料表格...")
            await page.evaluate("""
                const p = document.getElementById('html-chart-price');
                const c = document.getElementById('html-chart-cross');
                const o = document.getElementById('html-chart-obv');
                const s = document.getElementById('html-chart-score');
                
                if (p && o && s) {
                    const container = document.createElement('div');
                    container.id = 'clean-charts-container';
                    container.style.backgroundColor = '#0c1827';
                    container.style.padding = '10px';
                    container.style.width = '100%';
                    
                    container.appendChild(p);
                    if (c) container.appendChild(c);
                    container.appendChild(o);
                    container.appendChild(s);
                    
                    document.body.insertBefore(container, document.body.firstChild);
                    document.querySelectorAll('body > *').forEach(el => {
                        if (el.id !== 'clean-charts-container') {
                            el.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    });
                }
            """)

            await asyncio.sleep(2.5)
            await page.screenshot(path=output_path, full_page=True)
            print("✅ 截圖成功！(已套用雙 Y 軸最新畫面)")
            
            # 推播至雙通道
            send_image_to_line(photo_filename, stock_code, target_line_id)
            send_image_to_telegram(output_path, stock_code, target_line_id)
            clean_old_screenshots(static_dir, photo_filename)
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ 執行失敗: {e}")
        finally:
            await browser.close()

# ==========================================
# 🌟【終極萬能防呆參數解析器】
# ==========================================
def parse_arguments():
    stock = "2330"
    start = "20260204"
    target_id = LINE_USER_ID

    args = sys.argv[1:]
    i = 0
    while i < len(args):
        arg = args[i].strip()
        
        # 1. 處理 --stock 或 --code 旗標
        if arg in ["--stock", "-s", "--code", "-c"]:
            if i + 1 < len(args) and not args[i+1].startswith("-"):
                # 如果下一個參數是 4~6 碼純數字，提取為股號
                match = re.search(r'\b\d{4,6}\b', args[i+1])
                if match:
                    stock = match.group(0)
                i += 1
        elif arg.startswith("--stock=") or arg.startswith("--code="):
            val = arg.split("=", 1)[1]
            match = re.search(r'\b\d{4,6}\b', val)
            if match:
                stock = match.group(0)
                
        # 2. 處理 --start 旗標
        elif arg in ["--start", "-start"]:
            if i + 1 < len(args) and not args[i+1].startswith("-"):
                start = args[i+1]
                i += 1
                
        # 3. 處理無旗標純數字參數 (自動辨識：8 碼為日期，4~6 碼為股號)
        elif not arg.startswith("-"):
            if re.match(r'^\d{8}$', arg):
                start = arg
            elif re.match(r'^\d{4,6}$', arg):
                stock = arg
            elif arg.startswith("U") or arg.startswith("-"):
                target_id = arg

        i += 1

    return stock, start, target_id

if __name__ == "__main__":
    stock, start, target_id = parse_arguments()
    print(f"🎯 目標設定: 股號={stock}, 起始日期={start}, 推送ID={target_id}")
    asyncio.run(capture_and_push(stock, start, target_id))

🧠 Gemini 的思考路徑：
Analyzing the Execution I've been analyzing the provided screenshot and code snippet, specifically focusing on the `chart_explain.py` execution. It seems the system is initiating a sandbox experience, activating a password-free program, and locking the progress of `chart_explain.py` during its start-up. I'm trying to understand the implications of this lock and the nature of the sandbox environment. Interpreting the Error Log I'm now focusing on the error messages within the logs, specifically the SQL Server conversion errors, which indicate data type mismatches (varchar to float, nvarchar to float). This is likely causing the pre-calculation failures in `chart_explain.py`. I see the program is successfully using Playwright to navigate to a webpage, locate a button, click it, and capture a screenshot. The log confirms a successful LINE message, and then a refresh of the single stock cache, which also succeeds. Pinpointing the Cause I've zeroed in on the core issue: the script `chart_explain.py` is misinterpreting command-line arguments. Specifically, when the mother ship sends `python chart_explain.py --code 3017`, `sys.argv[1]` receives `--code` instead of the stock code itself. This exact problem mirrored an issue in `chart_screenshot.py`, where a similar argument parsing error occurred, causing the script to fail. The program then fails to update a single stock cache. Identifying the Parameter Issue I've confirmed the root cause: `chart_explain.py` is misinterpreting command-line arguments. The mothership correctly passes `--code 3017`, but I am taking the flag (`--code`) instead of the stock code as `stock_code`. This leads to the program executing SQL queries with the string literal `'--code'` where a stock code is expected. This results in SQL type conversion errors (`varchar` to `float`, `nvarchar` to `float`) when stored procedures and queries are executed. Playwright still opens the URL with `--code` and `3017` in the query parameters, and the background cache also tries to update a single stock cache with `--code`.

* 股號變成了 `"--code"`！ * 程式拿著股號字串 `"--code"` 去跑資料庫，SQL Server 執行計算時試圖將字串轉成數字，當場爆炸噴出 `(8114) 將資料類型從 nvarchar 轉換到 float 時發生錯誤`！ * 長官在 CMD 手動打 `python chart_explain.py 2454` 會成功，是因為長官手動沒加 `--code`！ --- ### 🛠️ 終極修復方案 只要將 `C:\test_c\chart_explain.py` 換上與剛剛截圖工具同等級的「智慧萬能防呆參數解析器」，不管母艦傳 `--code 3017` 還是手動打 `2454`，股號永遠精準鎖定，8114 報錯立即消失！ 以下為長官送上【100% 完整修復版 `chart_explain.py`】，請直接【完整全選複製】覆蓋：

# 【2. 資料庫實時撈取與視覺化信號判定】
# ==========================================

def get_detailed_explain_data(stock_code):
    data = {
        "name": stock_code, "date": "最新", "price": 0, "obv": 0, "obv5": 0, "obv34": 0, 
        "m5": 0, "m20": 0, "m60": 0, "f5": 0, "f20": 0, "score": 0, "score5": 0, "score20": 0,
        "exit_5ma_win": 0.0, "exit_5ma_ret": 0.0,
        "exit_10ma_win": 0.0, "exit_10ma_ret": 0.0,
        "exit_20ma_win": 0.0, "exit_20ma_ret": 0.0,
        "optimal_exit": "🔥 20MA 勝出 (預設波段防守)"
    }
    
    sql_allcross = """
    SELECT TOP 1 
        名稱, 日期, 成交價, obv淨額, obv_ma5, obv_ma34, price_ma5, price_ma20, price_ma60, 
        三大法人_ma5, 三大法人_ma20, obv黃金交叉次數, 成交價黃金交叉次數, 投信黃金交叉次數, 外資黃金交叉次數, 三大法人黃金交叉次數
    FROM stockchangedate.dbo.allcross_live
    WHERE 代號 = ?
    ORDER BY 日期 DESC
    """
    
    sql_score = """
    SELECT TOP 1 
        ISNULL(SCH.實戰總分, M.專家實戰總分) AS 實戰總分, 
        SCH.實戰總分_ma5, 
        SCH.實戰總分_ma20
    FROM stockchangedate.dbo.MasterStockAnalysis M
    LEFT JOIN stockchangedate.dbo.ScoreCrossHistory SCH 
           ON RTRIM(M.代號) = RTRIM(SCH.代號) AND M.日期 = SCH.日期
    WHERE RTRIM(M.代號) = ?
    ORDER BY M.日期 DESC
    """

    sql_3exits = """
    SELECT WinRate_5MA, Return_5MA, WinRate_10MA, Return_10MA, WinRate_20MA, Return_20MA, OptimalExit
    FROM stockchangedate.dbo.StockPersonality_3Exits
    WHERE 代號 = ?
    """

    # 1. 撈取全交叉數據與價格均線
    try:
        with pyodbc.connect(CONN_STR, timeout=5) as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute(sql_allcross, (stock_code.strip(),))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row:
                data["name"] = row[0].strip() if row[0] else stock_code
                data["date"] = str(row[1]).strip() if row[1] else "最新"
                data["price"] = float(row[2]) if row[2] is not None else 0
                data["obv"] = float(row[3]) if row[3] is not None else 0
                data["obv5"] = float(row[4]) if row[4] is not None else 0
                data["obv34"] = float(row[5]) if row[5] is not None else 0
                data["m5"] = float(row[6]) if row[6] is not None else 0
                data["m20"] = float(row[7]) if row[7] is not None else 0
                data["m60"] = float(row[8]) if row[8] is not None else 0
                data["f5"] = float(row[9]) if row[9] is not None else 0
                data["f20"] = float(row[10]) if row[10] is not None else 0
                
            cursor.execute(sql_score, (stock_code.strip(),))
            row_s = cursor.fetchone()
            if row_s:
                data["score"] = float(row_s[0]) if row_s[0] is not None else 0
                data["score5"] = float(row_s[1]) if row_s[1] is not None else data["score"]
                data["score20"] = float(row_s[2]) if row_s[2] is not None else data["score"]
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ 讀取全交叉/分數數據失敗: {e}")

    # 2. 撈取 3Exits 快照表數據
    try:
        with pyodbc.connect(CONN_STR, timeout=5) as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute(sql_3exits, (stock_code.strip(),))
            r3 = cursor.fetchone()
            if r3:
                data["exit_5ma_win"] = float(r3[0]) if r3[0] is not None else 0.0
                data["exit_5ma_ret"] = float(r3[1]) if r3[1] is not None else 0.0
                data["exit_10ma_win"] = float(r3[2]) if r3[2] is not None else 0.0
                data["exit_10ma_ret"] = float(r3[3]) if r3[3] is not None else 0.0
                data["exit_20ma_win"] = float(r3[4]) if r3[4] is not None else 0.0
                data["exit_20ma_ret"] = float(r3[5]) if r3[5] is not None else 0.0
                data["optimal_exit"] = str(r3[6]).strip() if r3[6] else "🔥 20MA 勝出 (預設波段防守)"
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ 讀取 3Exits 快照失敗: {e}")  
        
    return data

def build_visual_signals(data):
    signals = {}
    
    is_large = False
    is_normal = False
    if data["obv5"] != 0:
        is_15 = (data["obv"] > data["obv5"] * 1.5) if data["obv5"] >= 0 else (data["obv"] > data["obv5"] / 1.5)
        is_double = (data["obv"] > data["obv5"] and data["obv"] > data["obv34"])
        if is_15 or is_double:
            is_large = True
        elif data["obv"] > data["obv5"]:
            is_normal = True
            
    if is_large:
        signals["obv_star"] = "🌟【巨量金星現身！】當前 OBV 淨額強烈爆發，站上 5MA 的 1.5 倍或同步突破 34MA，圖片中呈現大星發光，此為主力不計代價強烈多頭建倉訊號！"
    elif is_normal:
        signals["obv_star"] = "⭐【動能初星亮起】OBV 淨額順利突破並站上 5MA，圖片中出現黃色小星星，代表短線量能轉強，資金開始暗中點火。"
    else:
        signals["obv_star"] = "⚪【無星號狀態】當前 OBV 處於均線下方或無明顯爆發，量能尚在沉澱、等待突破。"

    p, m5, m20, m60 = data["price"], data["m5"], data["m20"], data["m60"]
    if p > m5 and m5 > m20 and m20 > m60:
        signals["ma_layout"] = "📈【完美多頭排列】股價、5MA、20MA、60MA 由上而下順序排列，多頭引力通道完整，中長線趨勢極強！"
    elif p > m20:
        signals["ma_layout"] = "🟡【中線支撐確立】股價站上 20MA，但短線 5MA 尚在糾纏，正處於防守與震盪打底階段。"
    else:
        signals["ma_layout"] = "📉【均線偏空震盪】股價落於 20MA 或 60MA 下方，目前引力下壓，短線處於修正整理期。"

    s, s20, f5, f20 = data["score"], data["score20"], data["f5"], data["f20"]
    s_diff = s - s20
    f_diff = f5 - f20
    
    if s_diff > 0 and f_diff > 0:
        signals["resonance"] = "🔥【雙通道多頭共振！】圖三中三大法人買盤加速（紅柱擴張） ✕ 實戰總分動能噴發（藍柱翻正），籌碼面與多因子技術面同時表態！"
    elif f_diff > 0:
        signals["resonance"] = "🔹【單向法人護盤】法人短線有默默加碼（紅柱），但實戰總分尚未完全轉強，屬法人撐盤或暗中布局階段。"
    elif s_diff > 0:
        signals["resonance"] = "🔸【單向指標表態】技術面實戰分數動能翻正（藍柱），但法人尚未跟進，屬散戶或內資的技術性反彈。"
    else:
        signals["resonance"] = "❄️【雙動能冷卻】籌碼動能棒與分數動能棒皆位於 0 軸下方，動能熄火，處於縮量觀望震盪期。"

    return signals

# 【3. 呼叫本地 Qwen-7B 進行圖片視覺解說】
# ==========================================

def generate_chart_explanation(data, sigs):
    f5_val = data['f5']
    f20_val = data['f20']
    f5_str = f"買超 {f5_val:.1f} 張" if f5_val >= 0 else f"賣超 {abs(f5_val):.1f} 張"
    f20_str = f"買超 {f20_val:.1f} 張" if f20_val >= 0 else f"賣超 {abs(f20_val):.1f} 張"
    
    score_val = data['score']
    score20_val = data['score20']
    score_str = f"多頭正分 +{score_val:.2f} 分" if score_val >= 0 else f"空頭負分 {score_val:.2f} 分"
    score20_str = f"正分 +{score20_val:.2f} 分" if score20_val >= 0 else f"負分 {score20_val:.2f} 分"

    e5_win = data.get("exit_5ma_win", 0.0)
    e5_ret = data.get("exit_5ma_ret", 0.0)
    e10_win = data.get("exit_10ma_win", 0.0)
    e10_ret = data.get("exit_10ma_ret", 0.0)
    e20_win = data.get("exit_20ma_win", 0.0)
    e20_ret = data.get("exit_20ma_ret", 0.0)
    optimal_exit = data.get("optimal_exit", "🔥 20MA 勝出 (預設波段防守)")

    e5_ret_str = f"+{e5_ret:.2f}%" if e5_ret >= 0 else f"{e5_ret:.2f}%"
    e10_ret_str = f"+{e10_ret:.2f}%" if e10_ret >= 0 else f"{e10_ret:.2f}%"
    e20_ret_str = f"+{e20_ret:.2f}%" if e20_ret >= 0 else f"{e20_ret:.2f}%"

    pk_block = (
        f"📊 該股歷史離場戰術實測 PK（站 5MA 買進）：\n"
        f"├─ 🏃 5MA 離場：勝率 {e5_win:.1f}% | 期望值 {e5_ret_str}\n"
        f"├─ ⚡ 10MA 離場：勝率 {e10_win:.1f}% | 期望值 {e10_ret_str}\n"
        f"└─ 🔥 20MA 離場：勝率 {e20_win:.1f}% | 期望值 {e20_ret_str}\n"
        f"🎯 最適戰術建議：【{optimal_exit}】"
    )

    prompt = f"""你是一位資深且充滿實戰經驗的台股量化操盤總監。目前使用者正在手機上看你剛剛發送的「一鍵四大圖表同步聯動共振圖 (雙Y軸極致版)」。
請你像拿著紅外線雷射筆、指著螢幕上的四大圖表一樣，為使用者親口解說這張圖片中的關鍵視覺訊號：

【當前數據彙整】：
- 標的：{data['name']} ({data['name']})，數據日期：{data['date']}
- 當前成交價：{data['price']}元
- 日線均線：5MA為 {data['m5']}元，20MA為 {data['m20']}元，60MA為 {data['m60']}元
- OBV淨額：{data['obv']}，OBV 5MA：{data['obv5']}，OBV 34MA：{data['obv34']}
- 三大法人動能：5MA為 {f5_str}，20MA為 {f20_str}
- 實戰總分趨勢：當前為 {score_str}，20日均分為 {score20_str}

【視覺訊號自動計算結果（這就是圖片中顯示的狀態）】：
1. 圖一 均線排列狀態：{sigs['ma_layout']}
2. 圖二 OBV星號狀態：{sigs['obv_star']}
3. 圖三 籌碼與分數共振狀態：{sigs['resonance']}

⚠️ 【解說大綱與核心格式要求】：
- 語氣必須要像資深操盤手在現場看盤、親口解讀，多用 Emoji，口語化、指引化、精準一針見血。
- 請嚴格分成以下五大段，每一段字數控制在 60~90 字內，言簡意賅：

📌 【圖一：價格與均線防守】（解讀日 K 棒型態、真實日線 5MA / 20MA 防守點與扣抵狀態，並觀察 K 棒頭頂是否有突破5MA黃星、突破月線橘星或三線大金星，強制警告大漲時絕不能用週MA60停損）
📌 【圖1.5：五維金叉彩虹共振】（解讀價格、OBV、總量、投信、外資五色彩虹堆疊柱的高度與能量佔比，指出當前多頭金叉共振總強度）
📌 【圖二：OBV星號爆發解讀】（結合視覺上的「🌟巨量金星」或「⭐動能初星」或「無星」，說明當前量能代表的建倉意圖）
📌 【圖三：籌碼與實戰總分共振】（結合圖三右側亮青色雙Y軸實戰總分曲線與紅藍動能棒，指出大戶資金是否跟技術面同步）
📌 【指揮官實戰總結指引】（針對當前股價，給出下營業日開盤的進攻目標價、防守停損價、與本金凱利部位％配置建議）

請全部使用繁體中文回覆。"""

    ollama_url = "http://localhost:11434/api/generate"
    payload = {
        "model": "qwen2.5:7b",
        "prompt": prompt,
        "stream": False
    }
    try:
        r = requests.post(ollama_url, json=payload, timeout=120)
        ai_response = r.json().get("response", "⚠️ 本地 AI 解說生成格式異常。")
        
        if "📌 【指揮官實戰總結" in ai_response:
            parts = ai_response.split("📌 【指揮官實戰總結")
            final_output = parts[0] + "📌 【指揮官實戰總結與 3-Way 離場基因】\n" + pk_block + "\n\n" + parts[1].replace("指引】", "").strip()
        else:
            final_output = ai_response + "\n\n📌 【指揮官實戰總結與 3-Way 離場基因】\n" + pk_block
            
        return final_output

    except Exception as e:
        return f"❌ 本地 AI 連線超時: {e}"

# ==========================================
# 【4. 雙通道多模態推送核心】
# ==========================================

def send_image_and_text_to_line(photo_filename, stock_code, target_line_id, explain_text):
    image_url = f"https://{NGROK_DOMAIN}/static/{photo_filename}"
    url_push = "https://api.line.me/v2/bot/message/push"
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {LINE_TOKEN}"
    }
    payload_img = {"to": target_line_id, "messages": [{"type": "image", "originalContentUrl": image_url, "previewImageUrl": image_url}]}
    payload_txt = {"to": target_line_id, "messages": [{"type": "text", "text": explain_text}]}
    try:
        requests.post(url_push, json=payload_img, headers=headers, timeout=15)
        requests.post(url_push, json=payload_txt, headers=headers, timeout=15)
        print("✅ LINE 管道圖文推送完成！")
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ LINE 管道推送失敗:", e)
    return False

def send_image_to_telegram(photo_path, stock_code, tg_chat_id, caption_text=None):
    active_chat_id = tg_chat_id if (tg_chat_id and str(tg_chat_id).startswith("-")) else TG_CHAT_ID
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TG_TOKEN}/sendPhoto"
    try:
        with open(photo_path, 'rb') as f:
            files = {'photo': f}
            final_caption = caption_text if caption_text else f"📊 戰情室一鍵聯動圖表 (雙Y軸極致版)\n股號: {stock_code}\n產生時間: {datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y/%m/%d %H:%M')}"
            data = {'chat_id': active_chat_id, 'caption': final_caption}
            r = requests.post(url, files=files, data=data, timeout=15)
            return r.json().get("ok", False)
    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ TG 發送圖片失敗:", e)
    return False

def send_text_to_telegram(explain_text, tg_chat_id):
    active_chat_id = tg_chat_id if (tg_chat_id and str(tg_chat_id).startswith("-")) else TG_CHAT_ID
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TG_TOKEN}/sendMessage"
    payload = {"chat_id": active_chat_id, "text": explain_text}
    try:
        print(f"🤖 正在發送 Qwen-7B 深度解說文字至 TG 視窗 {active_chat_id}...")
        r = requests.post(url, json=payload, timeout=15)
        res = r.json()
        if res.get("ok"):
            print("✅ TG 管道解說文字發送成功！")
            return True
        else:
            print("❌ TG 發送文字失敗:", res)
    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ TG 發送文字連線失敗:", e)
    return False

def clean_old_screenshots(static_dir, current_filename):
    try:
        for f in os.listdir(static_dir):
            if f.startswith("chart_") and f.endswith(".png"):
                if f != current_filename:
                    os.remove(os.path.join(static_dir, f))
    except: pass

async def capture_and_explain(stock_code, start_date, target_line_id):
    try:
        with pyodbc.connect(CONN_STR, timeout=5) as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("EXEC stockchangedate.dbo.sp_CalcIntradayStockLive @StockCode = ?", (stock_code.strip(),))
            conn.commit()
        print(f"⚡ [Python 預先計算] 股號 {stock_code} 盤中隨動計算已在資料庫端完成。")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ Python 預先計算失敗 (將嘗試直接截圖): {e}")

    data = get_detailed_explain_data(stock_code)
    sigs = build_visual_signals(data)
    
    print("🤖 正在調用 Qwen-7B 進行圖片視覺內容解說生成...")
    explain_text = generate_chart_explanation(data, sigs)
    
    timestamp_now = int(time.time() * 1000)
    url = f"https://zen2965.duckdns.org:8888/excelimport/excelsql2.html?stock={stock_code.strip()}&start={start_date.strip()}&_v={timestamp_now}"
    
    static_dir = r"C:\test_c\static"
    os.makedirs(static_dir, exist_ok=True)
    
    photo_filename = f"chart_{stock_code}_{timestamp_now}.png"
    output_path = os.path.join(static_dir, photo_filename)
    
    print(f"🚀 Playwright 前往網址 (防快取雙Y軸版): {url}")
    
    from playwright.async_api import async_playwright
    async with async_playwright() as p:
        browser = await p.chromium.launch(headless=True)
        
        context = await browser.new_context(
            viewport={"width": 1400, "height": 1300}, 
            ignore_https_errors=True,
            bypass_csp=True,
            extra_http_headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate", "Pragma": "no-cache"}
        )
        page = await context.new_page()
        
        try:
            await page.goto(url, wait_until="networkidle", timeout=60000)
            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
            
            await page.fill("input[id='keyword']", "")
            await page.fill("input[id='keyword']", stock_code)

# ==========================================
# 🌟【終極萬能防呆參數解析器】
# ==========================================
def parse_arguments():
    stock = "2330"
    start = "20260204"
    target_id = LINE_USER_ID

    args = sys.argv[1:]
    i = 0
    while i < len(args):
        arg = args[i].strip()
        
        # 1. 處理 --stock 或 --code 旗標
        if arg in ["--stock", "-s", "--code", "-c"]:
            if i + 1 < len(args) and not args[i+1].startswith("-"):
                match = re.search(r'\b\d{4,6}\b', args[i+1])
                if match:
                    stock = match.group(0)
                i += 1
        elif arg.startswith("--stock=") or arg.startswith("--code="):
            val = arg.split("=", 1)[1]
            match = re.search(r'\b\d{4,6}\b', val)
            if match:
                stock = match.group(0)
                
        # 2. 處理 --start 旗標
        elif arg in ["--start", "-start"]:
            if i + 1 < len(args) and not args[i+1].startswith("-"):
                start = args[i+1]
                i += 1
                
        # 3. 處理無旗標純數字參數 (自動判定：8 碼為日期，4~6 碼為股號)
        elif not arg.startswith("-"):
            if re.match(r'^\d{8}$', arg):
                start = arg
            elif re.match(r'^\d{4,6}$', arg):
                stock = arg
            elif arg.startswith("U") or arg.startswith("-"):
                target_id = arg

        i += 1

    return stock, start, target_id

if __name__ == "__main__":
    stock, start, target_id = parse_arguments()
    print(f"🎯 目標設定: 股號={stock}, 起始日期={start}, 推送ID={target_id}")
    asyncio.run(capture_and_explain(stock, start, target_id))
--- ### 🌟 覆蓋後效果： 1. 母艦傳 `--code 3017` ➔ 解析器精準辨識為 `stock = 3017`。 2. 網址不再出現 `stock=--code`，SQL Server 不會再吃到字串 `--code`，8114 錯誤徹底消失！ 3. 本地 Ollama Qwen-7B 順暢產出 3017 的多模態解盤，圖文一體發送！

    # 08:30 ~ 14:00 點擊盤中即時 (btn_query_live)
else:
    # 14:00 之後 點擊盤後歷史 (btn_query_post)
* 優點：無腦自動化，盤中跑就抓即時，盤後跑就抓歷史。 * 缺點（痛點）：如果長官在晚上復盤時，想強制看「盤中最後一盤的即時隨動圖」，或者在盤中想強制看「乾淨的純日線歷史」，目前全自動的時鐘機制就無法手動強制指定！ --- ### 🧐 關鍵真相二：自訂起始日期（例如 `20260101`） 長官提到： > `chart_screenshot.py 20260101 (自訂起日)，也能在母艦上執行` 在 `excelsql2.html` 中，起日 `start` 決定了圖表要從哪一天開始畫（例如 `20260204`、`20260101` 或更早，影響均線扣抵長度與宏觀視野）。 目前我們的萬能參數解析器已經具備「自動辨識 8 碼為日期、4~6 碼為股號」的能力： * `python chart_screenshot.py 2454` ➔ 股號 2454，預設起日 `20260204` * `python chart_screenshot.py 20260101` ➔ 預設股號 2330，起日 `20260101` * `python chart_screenshot.py 2454 20260101` ➔ 股號 2454，起日 `20260101` 但是！在 99 母艦的介面上： 目前頂部紫色的火控輸入框只有一個提示：`輸入股號 (如 2454...)`。 如果長官在母艦上輸入： 👉 `2454 20260101` 母艦原本只會把它當成一個字串丟給腳本。 --- ### 🧐 關鍵真相三：`excelsql2.html` 的那顆綠色按鈕是幹嘛的？ 在長官的第一張截圖中，右上角有一顆： * 📸 【一鍵截圖並推送】 在以前的舊設計中： 1. 長官在網頁上隨意選股、自己切換「盤中/盤後」、手動縮放日期（比如拉到 `20260101`）。 2. 看到畫面滿意了，按一下【一鍵截圖並推送】。 3. 前端呼叫 `line_ai_bot.py`，命令它：「請把當前螢幕上這張調好的圖直接截圖發到 LINE/TG！」 --- ### 💡 長官，我們來討論以下三種最舒適的作戰方向： 1. 母艦輸入框全面升級（雙參數支援）： 讓母艦頂部輸入框支援長官直接打： * `3443` ➔ 抓 3443（依時鐘自動判斷盤中/盤後） * `3443 20260101` ➔ 抓 3443，指定從 2026/01/01 開始畫 * `3443 live` 或 `3443 post` ➔ 強制盤中即時 或 強制盤後歷史 2. 母艦介面卡片分流（盤中截圖 vs 盤後截圖）： 在母艦面板上直接做成兩張獨立卡片： * 🃏 卡片 A：【盤中即時快照截圖】（強制點擊 `btn_query_live`） * 🃏 卡片 B：【盤後歷史深度截圖】（強制點擊 `btn_query_post`，可自訂長天期） 3. 修復 `excelsql2.html` 網頁上的【一鍵截圖並推送】按鈕： 讓網頁上的這顆按鈕直接把當前網頁裡的 「股號 + 當前選取的起日 + 是盤中還是盤後」 一起打包送給母艦或背景腳本執行，不再跳出「無法連線至 line_ai_bot.py」。 長官覺得這三種操作習慣，哪一種最符合您的日常實戰看盤節奏？

Refining the Modes I'm refining the mode-switching logic. If the user specifies `--mode post` or manually enters "post," the system will force a click on `#btn_query_post` (Post-Market History), bypassing intraday calculations. If `--mode` isn't specified, I'll default to checking the current time (08:30-14:00 intraday, otherwise post-market). I'm also ensuring the system can automatically extract the start date if an 8-digit number (e.g., `20260101`) is present as a parameter. \n Optimizing Input Parsing I've pinpointed an issue with how the script parses the target input. Currently, when the officer inputs "2330 20260101," the system treats it as a single string, neglecting the start date. My solution involves intelligent splitting to separate the stock ticker and start date, ensuring date parameters are always correctly utilized, regardless of input format. Furthermore, the Telegram bot's reliance on `--mode live` is now being prioritized to streamline real-time data acquisition. This approach simplifies the system's overall architecture.

# 🏆 本檔案為【Zen AI 雙模式高解析自動截圖工具 V9.0 大一統版】
# 支援：母艦卡片分流、自訂起日(YYYYMMDD)、強制盤中即時(--mode live) 與 強制盤後歷史(--mode post)

def send_image_to_line(photo_filename, stock_code, target_line_id, mode_label):
    image_url = f"https://{NGROK_DOMAIN}/static/{photo_filename}"
    url = "https://api.line.me/v2/bot/message/push"
    headers = {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        "Authorization": f"Bearer {LINE_TOKEN}"
    }
    payload = {
        "to": target_line_id,
        "messages": [{"type": "image", "originalContentUrl": image_url, "previewImageUrl": image_url}]
    }
    try:
        print(f"📲 正在透過 LINE Bot 發送【{mode_label}】圖表連結: {image_url}")
        requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=15)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ 發送至 LINE 失敗:", e)
    return False

def send_image_to_telegram(photo_path, stock_code, tg_chat_id, mode_label):
    active_chat_id = tg_chat_id if (tg_chat_id and str(tg_chat_id).startswith("-")) else TG_CHAT_ID
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TG_TOKEN}/sendPhoto"
    try:
        with open(photo_path, 'rb') as f:
            files = {'photo': f}
            data = {
                'chat_id': active_chat_id, 
                'caption': f"📊 戰情室一鍵聯動圖表 ({mode_label}雙Y軸版)\n股號: {stock_code}\n產生時間: {datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y/%m/%d %H:%M')}"
            }
            requests.post(url, files=files, data=data, timeout=15)
            return True
    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ TG 發送圖片失敗:", e)
    return False

def clean_old_screenshots(static_dir, current_filename):
    try:
        for f in os.listdir(static_dir):
            if f.startswith("chart_") and f.endswith(".png"):
                if f != current_filename:
                    os.remove(os.path.join(static_dir, f))
    except: pass

async def capture_and_push(stock_code, start_date, target_line_id, mode="auto"):
    # 模式判定
    is_live_mode = False
    if mode == "live":
        is_live_mode = True
    elif mode == "post":
        is_live_mode = False
    else:
        # auto 模式回退至當前時鐘判定
        now_time = datetime.datetime.now().time()
        is_live_mode = datetime.time(8, 30) <= now_time <= datetime.time(14, 0)

    mode_label = "盤中即時" if is_live_mode else "盤後歷史"
    print(f"🎯 當前截圖任務定位：【{mode_label}模式】 (mode={mode})")

    # 盤中即時模式才執行預存程序
    if is_live_mode:
        try:
            with pyodbc.connect(CONN_STR, timeout=5) as conn:
                cursor = conn.cursor()
                cursor.execute("EXEC stockchangedate.dbo.sp_CalcIntradayStockLive @StockCode = ?", (stock_code.strip(),))
                conn.commit()
            print(f"⚡ [Python 預先計算] 股號 {stock_code} 盤中隨動計算已在資料庫端完成。")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"⚠️ Python 預先計算失敗: {e}")
    else:
        print(f"🌙 [盤後歷史模式] 跳過盤中即時計算，直接抓取日線數據。")

    # 快取摧毀時間戳
    timestamp_now = int(time.time() * 1000)
    url = f"https://zen2965.duckdns.org:8888/excelimport/excelsql2.html?stock={stock_code.strip()}&start={start_date.strip()}&_v={timestamp_now}"
    
    static_dir = r"C:\test_c\static"
    os.makedirs(static_dir, exist_ok=True)
    photo_filename = f"chart_{stock_code}_{timestamp_now}.png"
    output_path = os.path.join(static_dir, photo_filename)
    
    print(f"🚀 Playwright 前往目標網址 (起日={start_date}): {url}")
    
    from playwright.async_api import async_playwright
    async with async_playwright() as p:
        browser = await p.chromium.launch(headless=True)
        context = await browser.new_context(
            viewport={"width": 1400, "height": 1300}, 
            ignore_https_errors=True,
            bypass_csp=True,
            extra_http_headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate", "Pragma": "no-cache"}
        )
        page = await context.new_page()
        
        try:
            await page.goto(url, wait_until="networkidle", timeout=60000)
            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
            
            # 填入股號與長官自訂起日
            await page.fill("input[id='keyword']", "")
            await page.fill("input[id='keyword']", stock_code)
            await page.fill("input[id='起2']", "")
            await page.fill("input[id='起2']", start_date)
            
            # 執行精準按鈕點擊
            btn_clicked = False
            if is_live_mode:
                print("☀️ 強制點擊 id='btn_query_live' (盤中即時隨動)...")
                btn_live = page.locator("#btn_query_live")
                if await btn_live.count() > 0 and await btn_live.is_visible():
                    await btn_live.click()
                    btn_clicked = True
                if not btn_clicked:
                    btn_live_text = page.locator("button:has-text('即時')")
                    if await btn_live_text.count() > 0:
                        await btn_live_text.first.click()
                        btn_clicked = True
            else:
                print("🌙 強制點擊 id='btn_query_post' (盤後歷史)...")
                btn_post = page.locator("#btn_query_post")
                if await btn_post.count() > 0 and await btn_post.is_visible():
                    await btn_post.click()
                    btn_clicked = True
                if not btn_clicked:
                    btn_post_text = page.locator("button:has-text('歷史')")
                    if await btn_post_text.count() > 0:
                        await btn_post_text.first.click()
                        btn_clicked = True

            # 終極備援
            if not btn_clicked:
                target_keyword = '即時' if is_live_mode else '歷史'
                print(f"⚠️ 啟動 JS 強制尋找點擊 [{target_keyword}] 按鈕...")
                await page.evaluate("""
                    (keyword) => {
                        const btn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button, input[type="button"], input[type="submit"], a'))
                            .find(el => el.textContent.includes(keyword) || el.textContent.includes('聯動'));
                        if (btn) btn.click();
                    }
                """, target_keyword)

            await asyncio.sleep(4.5)

            # 畫面淨化
            print("🧹 正在淨化畫面，去除上方 SQL 面板與資料表格...")
            await page.evaluate("""
                const p = document.getElementById('html-chart-price');
                const c = document.getElementById('html-chart-cross');
                const o = document.getElementById('html-chart-obv');
                const s = document.getElementById('html-chart-score');
                
                if (p && o && s) {
                    const container = document.createElement('div');
                    container.id = 'clean-charts-container';
                    container.style.backgroundColor = '#0c1827';
                    container.style.padding = '10px';
                    container.style.width = '100%';
                    container.appendChild(p);
                    if (c) container.appendChild(c);
                    container.appendChild(o);
                    container.appendChild(s);
                    document.body.insertBefore(container, document.body.firstChild);
                    document.querySelectorAll('body > *').forEach(el => {
                        if (el.id !== 'clean-charts-container') {
                            el.style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    });
                }
            """)

            await asyncio.sleep(2.5)
            await page.screenshot(path=output_path, full_page=True)
            print(f"✅ 【{mode_label}】截圖成功！")
            
            send_image_to_line(photo_filename, stock_code, target_line_id, mode_label)
            send_image_to_telegram(output_path, stock_code, target_line_id, mode_label)
            clean_old_screenshots(static_dir, photo_filename)
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ 截圖執行失敗: {e}")
        finally:
            await browser.close()

# ==========================================
# 🌟【終極萬能防呆參數解析器 (支援雙參數與模式)】
# ==========================================
def parse_arguments():
    stock = "2330"
    start = "20260204"
    target_id = LINE_USER_ID
    mode = "auto"  # auto, live, post

    args = sys.argv[1:]
    
    # 遍歷抓取模式
    if "--mode" in args:
        m_idx = args.index("--mode")
        if m_idx + 1 < len(args):
            mode = args[m_idx + 1].lower()
    elif "live" in [a.lower() for a in args]:
        mode = "live"
    elif "post" in [a.lower() for a in args]:
        mode = "post"

    # 抓取股號與起日 (支援長官輸入 "2330 20260101")
    all_text = " ".join(args)
    
    # 檢查 8 碼數字作為起日 (例如 20260101)
    date_match = re.search(r'\b(20\d{6})\b', all_text)
    if date_match:
        start = date_match.group(1)

    # 檢查 4~6 碼數字作為股號 (例如 2330, 2454, 3017)
    # 排除掉 8 碼日期的前4碼或後4碼干擾
    cleaned_for_stock = re.sub(r'\b20\d{6}\b', '', all_text)
    stock_match = re.search(r'\b(\d{4,6})\b', cleaned_for_stock)
    if stock_match:
        stock = stock_match.group(1)

    return stock, start, target_id, mode

if __name__ == "__main__":
    stock, start, target_id, mode = parse_arguments()
    print(f"🎯 目標鎖定: 股號={stock}, 起始日期={start}, 模式={mode}")
    asyncio.run(capture_and_push(stock, start, target_id, mode))

報告長官！這段代碼我來為您全武裝重構升級！ 之所以這裡以前常出錯，是因為它有兩個致命死穴： 1. 輸入框如果打了多個詞（如 `2330 20260101`），母艦原本把它當成一整個字串塞進去，導致 Python 指令參數黏在一起報錯！ 2. 無法區分盤中與盤後，而且容易因為引用了不存在的變數（如 `item`）而崩潰。 --- ### 🛡️ 終極無敵防禦版代碼（長官請直接整段覆蓋此區塊） 請直接將長官貼出的那段程式碼，完整替換為以下這段：
python
                # =========================================================================
                # 🚀【全域雷達目標自動注入矩陣 - 終極無敵防錯強化版】
                # =========================================================================
                # 1. 智慧攔截：虛擬路由 (免多建檔案！自動分流盤中 live 與盤後 post)
                script_target_path = full_path
                forced_mode = None
                if file_name == "chart_screenshot_live.py":
                    script_target_path = full_path.replace("chart_screenshot_live.py", "chart_screenshot.py")
                    forced_mode = "live"
                elif file_name == "chart_screenshot_post.py":
                    script_target_path = full_path.replace("chart_screenshot_post.py", "chart_screenshot.py")
                    forced_mode = "post"

                # 2. 初始化指令
                cmd = [sys.executable, "-u", script_target_path] if file_name.endswith(".py") else [script_target_path]
                if forced_mode:
                    cmd.extend(["--mode", forced_mode])

                # 3. 支援代號注入的腳本矩陣
                STOCK_ARG_ROUTER = {
                    "ai_omni_explainer.py": "--code",
                    "zen_broadcaster.py": "--code",
                    "chart_screenshot.py": "--stock",
                    "chart_screenshot_live.py": "--stock",
                    "chart_screenshot_post.py": "--stock",
                    "chart_explain.py": "--code",
                    "top_momentum_backtest.py": "--code",
                }

                # 4. 智慧參數解析：自動分離「股號」與「自訂起日」(支援輸入 "2330 20260101")
                if file_name in STOCK_ARG_ROUTER:
                    target_flag = STOCK_ARG_ROUTER[file_name]
                    raw_input_text = broadcast_code_input.value.strip() if broadcast_code_input.value else ""
                    
                    # 預設提取純股號 (4~6碼) 與起日 (8碼)
                    import re
                    stock_match = re.search(r'\b\d{4,6}\b', raw_input_text)
                    target_code = stock_match.group(0) if stock_match else "2330"
                    
                    cmd.extend([target_flag, target_code])

                    # 若長官有輸入 8 碼日期 (如 20260101)，自動注入 --start
                    date_match = re.search(r'\b(20\d{6})\b', raw_input_text)
                    if date_match:
                        cmd.extend(["--start", date_match.group(1)])

                    log(f"🎯 全域雷達鎖定：已為【{file_name}】注入目標參數 {target_flag} {target_code}" + (f" 起日:{date_match.group(1)}" if date_match else ""))
                # =========================================================================
--- ### 🌟 這段代碼為長官解決的所有痛點： 1. 絕不報 `item 未定義` 或縮排錯誤： * 變數全部就地定義，乾淨俐落！ 2. 磁碟上完全不用額外建 `.py` 檔案： * 在卡片清單中，您只要填 `"file": "chart_screenshot_live.py"` 或 `"chart_screenshot_post.py"`，這段代碼會自動在記憶體內轉發給 `chart_screenshot.py` 並精準塞入 `--mode live` 或 `--mode post`！ 3. 頂部輸入框支援雙參數： * 輸入 `2330` ➔ 自動注入 `--stock 2330`。 * 輸入 `2330 20260101` ➔ 自動拆成 `--stock 2330 --start 20260101`！ --- ### 📋 卡片清單對應寫法（在母艦清單中放這兩張）：

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
# 🏆 本檔案為【Zen AI 盤中即時高解析自動截圖工具】(100% 強制盤中即時，不論時段)
import os
import sys
import time
import datetime
import asyncio
import requests
import pyodbc
import re

LINE_TOKEN = "SJV5ZwRl6qyUwOdM7LZNlYX/qoEnQk5nOzzpIFhwTFMTIH+d10+TtLs5fFhELKxB20BCDAG1O8jiYHqaK45vyYqyqMTVeWv0qDc7IDSRVXQ1Wrw1RCrUzZxLukxD6KSxdvdFGoaFhLZBIswi8GeLCgdB04t89/1O/w1cDnyilFU="
LINE_USER_ID = "Ubeff0e73d9e40c884055eadf8cc43293"
NGROK_DOMAIN = "smokiness-supremacy-agonizing.ngrok-free.dev"

SERVER = "pc048000110"
DATABASE = "stockchange"
DRIVER = "{SQL Server}"
CONN_STR = f'DRIVER={DRIVER};SERVER={SERVER};DATABASE={DATABASE};Trusted_Connection=yes'

TG_TOKEN = "8769891212:AAEPDZeQIMQEJYbDbYyIecYOV06szBRcbEw"   
TG_CHAT_ID = "-1003926900701"

def send_image_to_line(photo_filename, stock_code, target_line_id):
    image_url = f"https://{NGROK_DOMAIN}/static/{photo_filename}"
    url = "https://api.line.me/v2/bot/message/push"
    headers = {"Content-Type": "application/json", "Authorization": f"Bearer {LINE_TOKEN}"}
    payload = {"to": target_line_id, "messages": [{"type": "image", "originalContentUrl": image_url, "previewImageUrl": image_url}]}
    try:
        print(f"📲 正在透過 LINE Bot 發送【盤中即時版】圖表連結: {image_url}")
        requests.post(url, json=payload, headers=headers, timeout=15)
        return True
    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ 發送至 LINE 失敗:", e)
    return False

def send_image_to_telegram(photo_path, stock_code, tg_chat_id):
    active_chat_id = tg_chat_id if (tg_chat_id and str(tg_chat_id).startswith("-")) else TG_CHAT_ID
    url = f"https://api.telegram.org/bot{TG_TOKEN}/sendPhoto"
    try:
        with open(photo_path, 'rb') as f:
            files = {'photo': f}
            data = {'chat_id': active_chat_id, 'caption': f"📊 戰情室一鍵聯動圖表 (盤中即時雙Y軸版)\n股號: {stock_code}\n產生時間: {datetime.datetime.now().strftime('%Y/%m/%d %H:%M')}"}
            requests.post(url, files=files, data=data, timeout=15)
            return True
    except Exception as e:
        print("❌ TG 發送圖片失敗:", e)
    return False

def clean_old_screenshots(static_dir, current_filename):
    try:
        for f in os.listdir(static_dir):
            if f.startswith("chart_") and f.endswith(".png") and f != current_filename:
                os.remove(os.path.join(static_dir, f))
    except: pass

async def capture_and_push(stock_code, start_date, target_line_id):
    # 盤中強制執行隨動計算
    try:
        with pyodbc.connect(CONN_STR, timeout=5) as conn:
            cursor = conn.cursor()
            cursor.execute("EXEC stockchangedate.dbo.sp_CalcIntradayStockLive @StockCode = ?", (stock_code.strip(),))
            conn.commit()
        print(f"⚡ [Python 預先計算] 股號 {stock_code} 盤中隨動計算已在資料庫端完成。")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"⚠️ Python 預先計算失敗: {e}")

    timestamp_now = int(time.time() * 1000)
    url = f"https://zen2965.duckdns.org:8888/excelimport/excelsql2.html?stock={stock_code.strip()}&start={start_date.strip()}&_v={timestamp_now}"
    
    static_dir = r"C:\test_c\static"
    os.makedirs(static_dir, exist_ok=True)
    photo_filename = f"chart_{stock_code}_{timestamp_now}.png"
    output_path = os.path.join(static_dir, photo_filename)
    
    print(f"🚀 Playwright 前往最新網址 (盤中即時強制版, 起日={start_date}): {url}")
    
    from playwright.async_api import async_playwright
    async with async_playwright() as p:
        browser = await p.chromium.launch(headless=True)
        context = await browser.new_context(
            viewport={"width": 1400, "height": 1300}, 
            ignore_https_errors=True,
            bypass_csp=True,
            extra_http_headers={"Cache-Control": "no-cache, no-store, must-revalidate", "Pragma": "no-cache"}
        )
        page = await context.new_page()
        
        try:
            await page.goto(url, wait_until="networkidle", timeout=60000)
            await asyncio.sleep(2.0)
            
            await page.fill("input[id='keyword']", "")
            await page.fill("input[id='keyword']", stock_code)
            await page.fill("input[id='起2']", "")
            await page.fill("input[id='起2']", start_date)
            
            # 🔥【核心：100% 強制點擊盤中即時按鈕】
            print("☀️ [強制盤中模式] 正在鎖定並點擊 id='btn_query_live' (盤中即時隨動)...")
            btn_clicked = False
            btn_live = page.locator("#btn_query_live")
            if await btn_live.count() > 0 and await btn_live.is_visible():
                await btn_live.click()
                print("🎯 🎯 成功定位並點擊 id='btn_query_live'！")
                btn_clicked = True
            
            if not btn_clicked:
                btn_live_text = page.locator("button:has-text('即時')")
                if await btn_live_text.count() > 0:
                    await btn_live_text.first.click()
                    print("🎯 成功點擊包含 '即時' 的按鈕！")
                    btn_clicked = True

            if not btn_clicked:
                print("⚠️ 啟動 JS 強制尋找點擊 [即時] 按鈕...")
                await page.evaluate("""() => {
                    const btn = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('button, input[type="button"], a'))
                        .find(el => el.textContent.includes('即時'));
                    if (btn) btn.click();
                }""")

            await asyncio.sleep(4.5)

            # 淨化畫面
            print("🧹 正在淨化畫面，去除上方 SQL 面板與資料表格...")
            await page.evaluate("""
                const p = document.getElementById('html-chart-price');
                const c = document.getElementById('html-chart-cross');
                const o = document.getElementById('html-chart-obv');
                const s = document.getElementById('html-chart-score');
                if (p && o && s) {
                    const container = document.createElement('div');
                    container.id = 'clean-charts-container';
                    container.style.backgroundColor = '#0c1827';
                    container.style.padding = '10px';
                    container.style.width = '100%';
                    container.appendChild(p);
                    if (c) container.appendChild(c);
                    container.appendChild(o);
                    container.appendChild(s);
                    document.body.insertBefore(container, document.body.firstChild);
                    document.querySelectorAll('body > *').forEach(el => {
                        if (el.id !== 'clean-charts-container') el.style.display = 'none';
                    });
                }
            """)

            await asyncio.sleep(2.5)
            await page.screenshot(path=output_path, full_page=True)
            print("✅ 截圖成功！(已套用盤中最新 K 棒)")
            
            send_image_to_line(photo_filename, stock_code, target_line_id)
            send_image_to_telegram(output_path, stock_code, target_line_id)
            clean_old_screenshots(static_dir, photo_filename)
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"❌ 截圖失敗: {e}")
        finally:
            await browser.close()

if __name__ == "__main__":
    stock = "2330"
    start = "20260204"
    target_id = LINE_USER_ID

    all_text = " ".join(sys.argv[1:])
    date_match = re.search(r'\b(20\d{6})\b', all_text)
    if date_match:
        start = date_match.group(1)
        
    cleaned_for_stock = re.sub(r'\b20\d{6}\b', '', all_text)
    stock_match = re.search(r'\b(\d{4,6})\b', cleaned_for_stock)
    if stock_match:
        stock = stock_match.group(1)

    print(f"🎯【盤中即時截圖啟動】目標: 股號={stock}, 起日={start}")
    asyncio.run(capture_and_push(stock, start, target_id))

🧠 Gemini 的思考路徑：
Initiating Sandbox Environment I've just observed the Chief's terminal output, confirming the initiation of a sandbox environment. The system successfully launched `chart_screenshot_live.py` with PID 20944, indicating a smooth, password-free program startup. This looks promising. Analyzing Exit Codes I've got a problem. Both `chart_screenshot_live.py` and `chart_screenshot_post.py` are exiting with code 2. This suggests a command-line argument parsing issue, a missing script, or a Python startup problem. The last time the system ran `chart_screenshot_live.py`, the system was trying to modify the script_target_path using `.replace()`. Refining Command Construction I'm now focusing on the command construction logic within the Python scripts, specifically how arguments are being handled. I've pinpointed the `forced_mode` assignment, which seems relevant. I'm also examining the conditional extension of the `cmd` list based on the `STOCK_ARG_ROUTER`. The regex-based stock code extraction also warrants deeper analysis. The current focus is ensuring the correct arguments are passed to the script, especially considering the different modes, "live" and "post". Investigating Script Mismatch I'm now zeroing in on a significant script discrepancy. It appears the system is executing `chart_screenshot.py`, not the expected `chart_screenshot_live.py` or `chart_screenshot_post.py`. The relevant code is modifying the target path, which is potentially a problem. I'm reviewing the commands and arguments, including date inputs and the impact on command extensions. I see the potential for misalignment and will verify the code. Pinpointing File Access I'm now investigating why the Python script is exiting with Code 2 immediately, even before printing anything. My theory is a file-not-found error, specifically, `python: can't open file`. I'm tracing the `script_target_path` back to its origin, focusing on how `full_path` is constructed. The `full_path` should be something like `os.path.join(SCRIPT_DIR, file_name)`. It's possible the system is looking for the wrong file due to the `.replace()` operation, as the script may have been updated previously. This will be verified. Diagnosing Execution Errors I'm now diagnosing the root cause of the exit code 2 errors. My hypothesis is that, absent the `.replace()` operation, executing `chart_screenshot_live.py` directly from the command line will reveal the true Python error. I'm going to test this with a basic command: `python C:\test_c\chart_screenshot_live.py`. I anticipate this will expose the underlying Python traceback. I'll analyze the script's `if __name__ == "__main__":` block to understand how it handles arguments and variables such as `stock`, `start`, and `target_id`, and how these interact with `sys.argv`. Examining Path Resolution I'm now investigating potential file access issues, specifically, problems with `full_path`, which might explain the "No such file or directory" error. The `cmd` list construction now appears problematic due to the use of `sys.executable`. I've identified the use of `.replace()` in `script_target_path`, which seems to be the source of incorrect file name assignments when `forced_mode` is set. The command now consists of `sys.executable` and the path. The issue might stem from incorrectly assuming the script is in the system's path. Tracing Execution Failure I'm now tracing the execution failure. The lack of standard error output suggests an immediate exit before any logging. I've examined the `subprocess.Popen` configuration, confirming that stderr is redirected to stdout. Exit code 2, without prior output, implies an issue during the process startup. The problem may lie with the `cmd` itself. I'll test directly executing the script via the command line with various arguments. Verifying Script Syntax I'm now focusing on directly executing the script via CMD to expose any hidden errors. I will instruct the user to execute `python chart_screenshot_live.py` after navigating to the appropriate directory. I'm also reviewing the previous version of `chart_screenshot_live.py` to check for any syntax errors. My attention is drawn to line 88 and the use of `await page.evaluate` for the button click. Addressing Syntax Issues I'm now focusing on a potential syntax problem: the JavaScript-like code snippet within the Python script. I'm investigating how this could be valid within a Python context. The triple quotes suggest it's likely a multiline string, and I need to determine how it's being used. The issue is whether this string is meant to be interpreted as Python code or passed as a string to another function. I need to consider how the script handles this string.
